	function printDiv(divName) {
     var printContents = document.getElementById(divName).innerHTML;
     var originalContents = document.body.innerHTML;

     document.body.innerHTML = printContents;

     window.print();

     document.body.innerHTML = originalContents;
}

  @keyframes mugrDbLoaderSpin{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
  @media(max-width:575.98px){.mugr-db-loader{padding:16px}.mugr-db-loader__card{width:min(100%,350px);min-width:0;padding:17px 18px;border-radius:17px}.mugr-db-loader__spinner{width:38px;height:38px;flex-basis:38px;border-width:5px}}
  @media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.mugr-db-loader,.mugr-db-loader__spinner{transition:none}.mugr-db-loader__spinner{animation-duration:1.5s}}

<div id="mugr-database-loader" class="mugr-db-loader" role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-hidden="true">
  <div class="mugr-db-loader__card">
    <span class="mugr-db-loader__spinner" aria-hidden="true"></span>
    <span class="mugr-db-loader__copy">
      <strong>กำลังโหลดข้อมูล</strong>
      <span>กำลังเชื่อมต่อฐานข้อมูล กรุณารอสักครู่…</span>
    </span>

<script id="mugr-database-loader-v1-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  var overlay=document.getElementById('mugr-database-loader');
  if(!overlay || window.__mugrDbLoaderInstalled) return;
  window.__mugrDbLoaderInstalled=true;
  // headerTop.php lives inside a high-level stacking context. Moving the
  // overlay directly under <body> lets it sit above mega menus and the fixed
  // header; otherwise the blur appears but the spinner card is hidden behind
  // the open navigation panel.
  if(document.body && overlay.parentNode!==document.body){
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);
  }
  var pending=0, showTimer=0, safetyTimer=0;

  function show(immediate){
    clearTimeout(showTimer); clearTimeout(safetyTimer);
    var reveal=function(){overlay.classList.add('is-visible');overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');};
    if(immediate) reveal(); else showTimer=setTimeout(reveal,180);
    safetyTimer=setTimeout(hide,30000);
  }
  function hide(){
    pending=0; clearTimeout(showTimer); clearTimeout(safetyTimer);
    overlay.classList.remove('is-visible');overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  }
  window.MUGRDatabaseLoader={show:function(){show(true);},hide:hide};

  function parsed(value){try{return new URL(value,window.location.href);}catch(e){return null;}}
  function isDatabaseUrl(value){
    var u=parsed(value); if(!u || u.origin!==window.location.origin) return false;
    var path=u.pathname.toLowerCase(), p=u.searchParams.get('p')||'', t=u.searchParams.get('t')||'', g=u.searchParams.get('g')||'', topic=u.searchParams.get('topic')||'';
    // Academic Calendar already owns an inline AJAX loader. Let that component
    // handle both its initial request and year changes without a second overlay.
    if(p==='academic-calendar' || /\/engine\/current-students\/detail\/getschedule\.php$/i.test(path)) return false;
    // Curriculum pages own their loading state, except t=6 (Honorary
    // Professors), which has no built-in loader and uses this shared overlay.
    if(p==='curriculum' && t!=='6') return false;
    if(p==='curriculum' && t==='6') return true;
    var currentUrl=parsed(window.location.href);
    var currentIsCurriculum=currentUrl
      && (currentUrl.searchParams.get('p')||'')==='curriculum'
      && (currentUrl.searchParams.get('t')||'')!=='6';
    if(currentIsCurriculum && /\/(get(?:faculty|level|curri|course)|programme-director)[^/]*\.php$/i.test(path)) return false;
    if(/\/(get(?:schedule|faculty|level|announce|curri|tuition)|abstract-modal|programme-director)[^/]*\.php$/i.test(path)) return true;
    if(p==='news' && t==='3') return true;
    if(p==='aboutUs' && (t==='5' || t==='6' || t==='7' || t==='8')) return true;
    if(topic==='curriculumOpened' || topic==='schedule' || topic==='howToBeStudent') return true;
    if(path.indexOf('/current-students/')!==-1 && (g==='3' || g==='4' || g==='7')) return true;
    if(path.indexOf('/staff/')!==-1 && (g==='1' || g==='2' || g==='3' || g==='4' || g==='5' || g==='7')) return true;
    if(path.indexOf('/alumni/')!==-1 && (g==='0' || (g==='7' && (t==='1' || t==='2')))) return true;
    return false;
  }

  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    if(e.defaultPrevented || e.button!==0 || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.altKey) return;
    var a=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('a[href]'); if(!a) return;
    if(a.target==='_blank' || a.hasAttribute('download') || a.getAttribute('href').charAt(0)==='#') return;
    if(a.hasAttribute('data-db-loader') || isDatabaseUrl(a.href)) show(true);
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('submit',function(e){
    var f=e.target;if(!f || f.nodeName!=='FORM') return;
    if(f.hasAttribute('data-db-loader') || isDatabaseUrl(f.action || window.location.href) || isDatabaseUrl(window.location.href)) show(true);
  },true);

  var originalOpen=XMLHttpRequest.prototype.open, originalSend=XMLHttpRequest.prototype.send;
  XMLHttpRequest.prototype.open=function(method,url){this.__mugrDbRequest=isDatabaseUrl(url);return originalOpen.apply(this,arguments);};
  XMLHttpRequest.prototype.send=function(){
    if(this.__mugrDbRequest){pending++;show(false);this.addEventListener('loadend',function(){pending=Math.max(0,pending-1);if(!pending)hide();},{once:true});}
    return originalSend.apply(this,arguments);
  };
  if(window.fetch){
    var originalFetch=window.fetch;
    window.fetch=function(input){
      var url=(typeof input==='string')?input:(input&&input.url)||'';
      if(!isDatabaseUrl(url)) return originalFetch.apply(this,arguments);
      pending++;show(false);
      return originalFetch.apply(this,arguments).then(function(result){return result;}).finally(function(){pending=Math.max(0,pending-1);if(!pending)hide();});
    };
  }
  window.addEventListener('pageshow',hide);
  window.addEventListener('pagehide',function(){clearTimeout(showTimer);});
})();
</script>

<script>
(function(){
 function setup(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.box-lang .mugr-language-capsule').forEach(function(capsule){
   if(capsule.dataset.heightSync)return;
   var row=capsule.closest('.box-right')||capsule.parentElement.parentElement;
   var reference=row.querySelector('.mugr-student-action-btn')||row.querySelector('.mugr-header-action-btn');
   if(!reference)return;
   capsule.dataset.heightSync='1';
   function update(){var h=reference.getBoundingClientRect().height;if(h>0)capsule.style.setProperty('--mugr-language-height',h+'px');}
   update();
   if(window.ResizeObserver){new ResizeObserver(update).observe(reference);}else{window.addEventListener('resize',update);}
   if(document.fonts&&document.fonts.ready)document.fonts.ready.then(update);
  });
 }
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',setup);else setup();
})();
</script>

<script>
(function(){
 function setup(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.box-lang .mugr-language-capsule').forEach(function(capsule){
   if(capsule.dataset.heightSync)return;
   var row=capsule.closest('.box-right')||capsule.parentElement.parentElement;
   var reference=row.querySelector('.mugr-student-action-btn')||row.querySelector('.mugr-header-action-btn');
   if(!reference)return;
   capsule.dataset.heightSync='1';
   function update(){var h=reference.getBoundingClientRect().height;if(h>0)capsule.style.setProperty('--mugr-language-height',h+'px');}
   update();
   if(window.ResizeObserver){new ResizeObserver(update).observe(reference);}else{window.addEventListener('resize',update);}
   if(document.fonts&&document.fonts.ready)document.fonts.ready.then(update);
  });
 }
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',setup);else setup();
})();
</script>

<h1>อัตลักษณ์มหาวิทยาลัยมหิดล</h1>
<p>ตราสัญลักษณ์ แม่แบบงานนำเสนอ และแนวทางใช้งานอัตลักษณ์ของมหาวิทยาลัยอย่างถูกต้อง</p>

<script>(function(){'use strict';
function initAboutLayout(){
 var sidebar=document.querySelector('.mugr-about-sidebar');if(!sidebar||sidebar.dataset.ready==='1')return;sidebar.dataset.ready='1';
 var sidebarShell=sidebar.closest('.mugr-about-sidebar-shell')||sidebar.parentNode;
 var section=sidebarShell.closest('section')||sidebarShell.parentNode;if(section)section.classList.add('mugr-about-page-shell');var main=section&&section.querySelector(':scope > main');
 if(!main&&section)main=section.querySelector('main');
 var heading=section&&section.querySelector(':scope > .mugr-about-page-heading');if(!heading&&section)heading=section.querySelector('.mugr-about-page-heading');
 var layout,contentColumn;
 if(main){layout=document.createElement('div');layout.className='mugr-about-content-layout';contentColumn=document.createElement('div');contentColumn.className='mugr-about-content-column';sidebarShell.parentNode.insertBefore(layout,sidebarShell);layout.appendChild(sidebarShell);layout.appendChild(contentColumn);if(heading)contentColumn.appendChild(heading);contentColumn.appendChild(main)}
 else{layout=sidebar.parentElement;layout.classList.add('mugr-about-content-layout')}
 var toggle=sidebar.querySelector('.mugr-about-sidebar__toggle');
 var storageKey='mugr-about-sidebar-state-v2';var saved='';try{saved=sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey)||''}catch(e){}
 // Default: show the About sidebar. Users can still collapse it manually,
 // and that choice is remembered for the rest of the browser session.
 var collapsed=saved==='collapsed';
 function apply(state,persist){collapsed=state;layout.classList.add('is-sidebar-transitioning');layout.classList.toggle('is-sidebar-collapsed',collapsed);if(toggle){toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded',collapsed?'false':'true');toggle.setAttribute('title',collapsed?'แสดงเมนู':'ซ่อนเมนู')}if(persist){try{sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey,collapsed?'collapsed':'expanded')}catch(e){}}window.setTimeout(function(){layout.classList.remove('is-sidebar-transitioning');stickyAnchorY=0;measureDesktopSticky();sync()},360)}
 apply(collapsed,false);if(toggle)toggle.addEventListener('click',function(){apply(!collapsed,true)});
 var mobile=document.querySelector('.mugr-about-mobile-nav'),sentinel=document.querySelector('.mugr-about-mobile-nav-sentinel'),spacer=document.querySelector('.mugr-about-mobile-nav-spacer');var buttons=[].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-about-group]')),panels=[].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-about-panel]'));

 var singleNav=mobile&&mobile.querySelector('[data-mugr-about-single]');
 var singleTrack=mobile&&mobile.querySelector('[data-mugr-about-single-track]');
 var singleActive=singleTrack&&singleTrack.querySelector('.mugr-about-mobile-nav__single-item.is-active');
 var more=mobile&&mobile.querySelector('[data-mugr-about-more]');
 var moreBtn=mobile&&mobile.querySelector('[data-mugr-about-more-btn]');
 var moreMenu=mobile&&mobile.querySelector('[data-mugr-about-more-menu]');

 function centerSingleActive(smooth){
   if(innerWidth>767||!singleTrack||!singleActive)return;
   var target=singleActive.offsetLeft+(singleActive.offsetWidth/2)-(singleTrack.clientWidth/2);
   var max=Math.max(0,singleTrack.scrollWidth-singleTrack.clientWidth);
   target=Math.max(0,Math.min(target,max));
   singleTrack.scrollTo({left:target,behavior:smooth?'smooth':'auto'});
 }
 function closeMore(){
   if(!more||!moreBtn||!moreMenu)return;
   more.classList.remove('is-open');
   moreBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
   moreMenu.hidden=true;
 }
 if(moreBtn){
   moreBtn.addEventListener('click',function(e){
     e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
     var open=!more.classList.contains('is-open');
     closeMore();
     if(open){
       more.classList.add('is-open');
       moreBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');
       moreMenu.hidden=false;
     }
   });
 }
 document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
   if(more&&!more.contains(e.target))closeMore();
 });
 document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
   if(e.key==='Escape'){closeMore();if(moreBtn)moreBtn.focus()}
 });

 function center(el,scroller,smooth){if(!el||!scroller)return;var r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),sr=scroller.getBoundingClientRect(),x=scroller.scrollLeft+(r.left+r.width/2-sr.left-sr.width/2),max=Math.max(0,scroller.scrollWidth-scroller.clientWidth);scroller.scrollTo({left:Math.max(0,Math.min(x,max)),behavior:smooth?'smooth':'auto'})}
 function activate(key,smooth){buttons.forEach(function(b){var on=b.getAttribute('data-about-group')===key;b.classList.toggle('is-active',on);b.setAttribute('aria-selected',on?'true':'false');if(on)center(b,b.parentNode,smooth)});panels.forEach(function(p){var on=p.getAttribute('data-about-panel')===key;p.classList.toggle('is-active',on);if(on){var a=p.querySelector('.is-active')||p.querySelector('a');setTimeout(function(){center(a,p.parentNode,smooth)},0)}})}buttons.forEach(function(b){b.addEventListener('click',function(){activate(b.getAttribute('data-about-group'),true)})});

 if(innerWidth<=767){
   window.requestAnimationFrame(function(){centerSingleActive(false)});
 }

 function visibleBottom(el,stickyOnly){if(!el||mobile&&mobile.contains(el))return 0;var st=getComputedStyle(el),r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(st.display==='none'||st.visibility==='hidden'||Number(st.opacity)===0||r.bottom<=0||r.top>=innerHeight||r.width<innerWidth*.45)return 0;if(stickyOnly&&st.position!=='fixed'&&st.position!=='sticky')return 0;return Math.max(0,Math.min(r.bottom,innerHeight))}
 function headerBottom(){var m=0,isDesktop=innerWidth>991,sel=isDesktop?'#masthead,.header-fixed,.site-header,.techmarket-sticky-wrap,.box-header-top,.box-header-bottom,.header-top,.header-bottom,.top.header-logo,.bottom.header-fixed':'.mugr-mnav32__header,.mugr-mnav32__brandrow,.mugr-mnav32__searchrow,.box-wsmobileheader';[].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll(sel),function(e){m=Math.max(m,visibleBottom(e,isDesktop))});if(isDesktop&&m>220)m=0;document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--mugr-about-header-bottom',Math.round(m)+'px');return Math.round(m)}
 var stickyAnchorY=0;
 function measureDesktopSticky(){
   if(innerWidth<=991||!sidebarShell||!sidebar||!layout)return;
   var wasFixed=sidebarShell.classList.contains('is-fixed');
   if(wasFixed)sidebarShell.classList.remove('is-fixed');
   sidebar.style.removeProperty('--mugr-about-sticky-shift');
   stickyAnchorY=sidebarShell.getBoundingClientRect().top+window.pageYOffset;
   if(wasFixed)sidebarShell.classList.add('is-fixed');
 }
 function syncDesktopSticky(top){
   if(!sidebarShell||!sidebar||!layout||innerWidth<=991){
     if(sidebarShell)sidebarShell.classList.remove('is-fixed');
     if(sidebarShell)sidebarShell.style.height='';
     return;
   }
   if(!stickyAnchorY)measureDesktopSticky();
   var stickyTop=Math.max(12,top+18);
   var shouldFix=window.pageYOffset+stickyTop>=stickyAnchorY;
   if(!shouldFix){sidebarShell.classList.remove('is-fixed');sidebarShell.style.height='';sidebar.style.removeProperty('--mugr-about-sticky-shift');return;}
   var shellRect=sidebarShell.getBoundingClientRect();
   sidebarShell.style.height=Math.ceil(sidebar.offsetHeight)+'px';
   sidebarShell.style.setProperty('--mugr-about-sticky-top',stickyTop+'px');
   sidebarShell.style.setProperty('--mugr-about-sticky-left',Math.round(shellRect.left)+'px');
   sidebarShell.style.setProperty('--mugr-about-sticky-width',Math.round(shellRect.width)+'px');
   sidebarShell.classList.add('is-fixed');
   var layoutBottom=layout.getBoundingClientRect().bottom;
   var cardHeight=Math.min(sidebar.scrollHeight,innerHeight-stickyTop-12);
   var shift=Math.min(0,layoutBottom-(stickyTop+cardHeight+12));
   sidebarShell.style.setProperty('--mugr-about-sticky-shift',Math.round(shift)+'px');
 }
 function sync(){var top=headerBottom();syncDesktopSticky(top);if(!mobile||!sentinel||!spacer||innerWidth>991){if(mobile)mobile.classList.remove('is-sticky');if(spacer)spacer.style.height='0';return}var sticky=sentinel.getBoundingClientRect().top<=top;if(sticky){mobile.classList.add('is-sticky');mobile.style.setProperty('--mugr-about-header-bottom',top+'px');spacer.style.height=Math.ceil(mobile.getBoundingClientRect().height)+'px'}else{mobile.classList.remove('is-sticky');mobile.style.removeProperty('--mugr-about-header-bottom');spacer.style.height='0'}}
 var queued=false;function queue(){if(queued)return;queued=true;requestAnimationFrame(function(){queued=false;sync()})}sync();var activeButton=document.querySelector('[data-about-group].is-active');if(activeButton)center(activeButton,activeButton.parentNode,false);var activePanel=document.querySelector('[data-about-panel].is-active'),activeLink=activePanel&&activePanel.querySelector('.is-active');if(activeLink)center(activeLink,activePanel.parentNode,false);addEventListener('scroll',queue,{passive:true});addEventListener('resize',function(){stickyAnchorY=0;measureDesktopSticky();queue()},{passive:true});addEventListener('orientationchange',function(){stickyAnchorY=0;setTimeout(function(){measureDesktopSticky();sync()},120)},{passive:true});addEventListener('load',function(){stickyAnchorY=0;measureDesktopSticky();sync()},{once:true})
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',initAboutLayout,{once:true});else initAboutLayout();
})();</script>

                    <p class="mugr-identity-kicker">BRAND ESSENTIALS</p>
                    <h2 id="mugr-identity-intro-title">ใช้งานอัตลักษณ์ให้ถูกต้องในทุกสื่อ</h2>
                    <p>เลือกดาวน์โหลดไฟล์จากแหล่งข้อมูลอย่างเป็นทางการของมหาวิทยาลัย และตรวจสอบรูปแบบที่เหมาะสมก่อนนำไปใช้กับงานนำเสนอ สิ่งพิมพ์ เว็บไซต์ หรือสื่อประชาสัมพันธ์</p>
                </div>
                <div class="mugr-identity-intro__seal" aria-hidden="true"><span>MU</span><small>WISDOM<br>OF THE LAND</small></div>
            </section>

            <section class="mugr-identity-resource-grid" aria-label="รายการดาวน์โหลด">

                            <span class="mugr-identity-resource__motif" aria-hidden="true"></span>

                <div class="mugr-identity-resource__body">
                    <p>CORPORATE IDENTITY MANUAL</p>
                    <h3 id="mugr-ci-manual-title">คู่มือระบบอัตลักษณ์มหาวิทยาลัยมหิดล</h3>
                    <div class="mugr-identity-resource__tags"><span>PDF</span><span>ฉบับปี 2559 (2016)</span><span>เอกสารภาษาไทย</span></div>
                    <div class="mugr-identity-resource__description">คู่มือแนวทางการใช้ตราสัญลักษณ์ สีอัตลักษณ์ และองค์ประกอบของอัตลักษณ์มหาวิทยาลัยมหิดล</div>
                    <a href="/download/main/CI-Mahidol.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><span>เปิดคู่มือ PDF</span><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M14 4h6v6M20 4l-9 9"/><path d="M18 13v6H5V6h6"/></svg></a>
                </div>
            </section>

            <section class="mugr-identity-guidance" aria-labelledby="mugr-identity-guidance-title">
                <header class="mugr-identity-section-head">
                    <div><p>USAGE GUIDANCE</p><h2 id="mugr-identity-guidance-title">หลักการใช้งานเบื้องต้น</h2></div>
                    <span><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M12 3 5 6v5c0 4.6 2.8 8.1 7 10 4.2-1.9 7-5.4 7-10V6l-7-3Z"/><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"/></svg></span>
                </header>
                <div class="mugr-identity-guidance__grid">
                    <article><span>01</span><div><h3>ใช้ไฟล์ต้นฉบับ</h3><p>ดาวน์โหลดจากหน้าอย่างเป็นทางการ ไม่คัดลอกจากภาพบนเว็บไซต์หรือเอกสารอื่น</p></div></article>
                    <article><span>02</span><div><h3>รักษาสัดส่วน</h3><p>ปรับขนาดโดยรักษาอัตราส่วนเดิม และเว้นพื้นที่รอบตราสัญลักษณ์ให้เหมาะสม</p></div></article>
                    <article><span>03</span><div><h3>เลือกรูปแบบให้เหมาะกับพื้นหลัง</h3><p>เลือกรูปแบบสีหรือขาวดำที่อ่านชัดเจน และมีความต่างของสีเพียงพอ</p></div></article>
                    <article><span>04</span><div><h3>ไม่ดัดแปลงองค์ประกอบ</h3><p>หลีกเลี่ยงการยืด บีบ หมุน เปลี่ยนสี หรือจัดวางส่วนประกอบใหม่</p></div></article>
                </div>
            </section>

            <section class="mugr-identity-official-note">
                <span><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="m5 12 4 4L19 6"/></svg></span>
                <div><h2>เชื่อมโยงกับแหล่งข้อมูลของมหาวิทยาลัยโดยตรง</h2><p>แม่แบบและตราสัญลักษณ์เชื่อมไปยังเว็บไซต์มหาวิทยาลัยมหิดล ส่วนคู่มือ PDF เป็นฉบับปี 2559 (2016) ที่เผยแพร่ไว้ในหน้านี้</p></div>
            </section>
        </div>

<script>
    const btn = document.getElementById('backToTop');
    const progressRing = btn ? btn.querySelector('.mugr-backtop-progress') : null;
    const updateBackToTop = () => {
      if (!btn) return;
      const scrollTop = window.scrollY || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0;
      const maxScroll = Math.max(0, document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight);
      const progress = maxScroll ? Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (scrollTop / maxScroll) * 100)) : 0;
      btn.classList.toggle('show', scrollTop > 300);
      btn.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Back to top — ' + Math.round(progress) + '%');
      if (progressRing) progressRing.style.strokeDashoffset = String(100 - progress);
    };
    window.addEventListener('scroll', updateBackToTop, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', updateBackToTop, { passive: true });
    updateBackToTop();
    if (btn) btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    });
  </script>

    <script>
      (function () {
        var MOBILE_MAX = 860;

        function getPanel(item) {
          return item ? item.querySelector('.mugr-footer-accordion-panel') : null;
        }

        function setPanelHeight(item, open, immediate) {
          var panel = getPanel(item);
          if (!panel) return;

          if (window.innerWidth > MOBILE_MAX) {
            panel.style.maxHeight = '';
            return;
          }

          if (immediate) panel.style.transition = 'none';
          if (open) {
            panel.style.maxHeight = panel.scrollHeight + 16 + 'px';
          } else {
            panel.style.maxHeight = '0px';
          }
          if (immediate) {
            panel.offsetHeight;
            panel.style.transition = '';
          }
        }

        function closeItem(item) {
          if (!item) return;
          var panel = getPanel(item);
          var toggle = item.querySelector('.mugr-footer-accordion-toggle');
          if (panel && window.innerWidth <= MOBILE_MAX) {
            panel.style.maxHeight = panel.scrollHeight + 'px';
            panel.offsetHeight;
            requestAnimationFrame(function () { panel.style.maxHeight = '0px'; });
          }
          item.classList.remove('is-open');
          if (toggle) toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        }

        function openItem(item) {
          if (!item) return;
          var panel = getPanel(item);
          var toggle = item.querySelector('.mugr-footer-accordion-toggle');
          item.classList.add('is-open');
          if (toggle) toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
          if (panel && window.innerWidth <= MOBILE_MAX) {
            panel.style.maxHeight = '0px';
            panel.offsetHeight;
            requestAnimationFrame(function () {
              panel.style.maxHeight = panel.scrollHeight + 16 + 'px';
            });
          }
        }

        function initMugrFooterAccordion() {
          var toggles = document.querySelectorAll('.footer .mugr-footer-accordion-toggle');
          for (var i = 0; i < toggles.length; i++) {
            (function (toggle) {
              if (toggle.getAttribute('data-mugr-accordion-ready') === '1') return;
              toggle.setAttribute('data-mugr-accordion-ready', '1');
              var item = toggle.closest('.col-link');
              if (item) setPanelHeight(item, item.classList.contains('is-open'), true);

              toggle.addEventListener('click', function () {
                if (window.innerWidth > MOBILE_MAX) return;
                var current = toggle.closest('.col-link');
                if (!current) return;
                var wasOpen = current.classList.contains('is-open');
                var siblings = current.parentNode ? current.parentNode.querySelectorAll('.col-link.is-open') : [];
                for (var j = 0; j < siblings.length; j++) {
                  if (siblings[j] !== current) closeItem(siblings[j]);
                }
                if (wasOpen) closeItem(current);
                else openItem(current);
              });
            })(toggles[i]);
          }
        }

        var resizeTimer;
        window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
          clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
          resizeTimer = setTimeout(function () {
            var items = document.querySelectorAll('.footer .col-link');
            for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
              var panel = getPanel(items[i]);
              if (!panel) continue;
              if (window.innerWidth > MOBILE_MAX) panel.style.maxHeight = '';
              else setPanelHeight(items[i], items[i].classList.contains('is-open'), true);
            }
          }, 120);
        });

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initMugrFooterAccordion);
        else initMugrFooterAccordion();
      })();
    </script>

    <script>
      (function () {
        var path = (window.location.pathname || '').toLowerCase();
        var tabs = document.querySelectorAll('.mugr-app-tabbar .mugr-app-tab');
        var activeSection = '';
        if (path.indexOf('/prospective-students/') !== -1) activeSection = 'prospective-students';
        else if (path.indexOf('/current-students/') !== -1) activeSection = 'current-students';
        else if (path.indexOf('/staff/') !== -1) activeSection = 'staff';
        else if (path.indexOf('/alumni/') !== -1) activeSection = 'alumni';
        for (var i = 0; i < tabs.length; i++) {
          var tab = tabs[i];
          var isActive = activeSection && tab.getAttribute('data-section') === activeSection;
          tab.classList.toggle('is-active', !!isActive);
          if (isActive) tab.setAttribute('aria-current', 'page'); else tab.removeAttribute('aria-current');
        }
      })();
    </script>

<script id="mugr-search-modal-v60-js">
(function(){

  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var chip=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('[data-search-keyword]'):null;
    if(!chip)return;
    var keyword=String(chip.getAttribute('data-search-keyword')||'').trim();
    if(keyword==='รายงานตัว'){
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      window.location.href='/thai/prospective-students/?announce=4';
    }
  },true);


  function mugrFixRetiredFormsRouteV51(url){
    var u=String(url||'');
    u=u.replace(/^https?:\/\/graduate\.mahidol\.ac\.th/i,'');
    if(u==='/thai/current-students/?g=10') return '/thai/?p=download-forms';
    if(u==='/inter/current-students/?g=10') return '/inter/?p=download-forms';
    // Curriculum moved to Prospective Students. Rewrite even if an old cached shortcut is still rendered.
    if(u==='/thai/?p=curriculum') return '/thai/prospective-students/?p=curriculum';
    if(u==='/inter/?p=curriculum') return '/inter/prospective-students/?p=curriculum';
    return url;
  }
  function mugrRewriteRetiredFormsLinksV51(root){
    root=root||document;
    var links=root.querySelectorAll ? root.querySelectorAll('a[href]') : [];
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(links,function(a){
      var fixed=mugrFixRetiredFormsRouteV51(a.getAttribute('href'));
      if(fixed!==a.getAttribute('href')) a.setAttribute('href',fixed);
    });
  }
  mugrRewriteRetiredFormsLinksV51(document);
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var a=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('a[href]'):null;
    if(!a)return;
    var old=a.getAttribute('href'), fixed=mugrFixRetiredFormsRouteV51(old);
    if(fixed!==old)a.setAttribute('href',fixed);
  },true);

  if(window.__mugrSearchModalV60) return;
  window.__mugrSearchModalV60 = true;

  var modal, input, resultsBox, statusBox, form, lastController;
  var lang = 'thai';
  var endpoint = '/engine/search/v5/api/frontend.php';
  var loadingTimer = null;
  var loadingStep = 0;
  var loadingPercent = 12;
  var loadingDotCount = 1;
  var loadingStartedAt = 0;
  var activeSearchId = 0;
  var MIN_LOADING_VISIBLE_MS = 1200;
  var SEARCH_INPUT_SELECTOR = 'input[name=\"q\"], input[type=\"search\"], [data-mugr-inline-search], #searchText';

  function closeLegacySuggestions(){
    if(window.MUGRGlobalSearch && typeof window.MUGRGlobalSearch.closeAll === 'function'){ try{ window.MUGRGlobalSearch.closeAll(); }catch(e){} }
    document.querySelectorAll('.mugr-inline-suggest, [data-mugr-inline-suggest], .mugr-search-suggest, .search-suggest, .autocomplete-suggestions, .ui-autocomplete, .mugr-global-search-suggest, [data-mugr-global-search-suggest], .mugr-global-search-floating').forEach(function(box){
      box.innerHTML = '';
      box.style.display = 'none';
      box.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    });
  }
  function isSearchInput(el){
    return !!(el && el.matches && el.matches(SEARCH_INPUT_SELECTOR));
  }
  function isDirectSearch(el){
    /* V61: do not treat header/breadcrumb global-search forms as direct-submit forms.
       They must open the Search Modal instead of navigating to /engine/search/v5/public/search.php.
       Keep only explicitly direct/home forms excluded. */
    return !!(el && el.closest && el.closest('[data-mugr-home-direct-search], [data-mugr-direct-search], [data-mugr-home-search-v233]'));
  }
  function isInsideModal(el){
    return !!(el && el.closest && el.closest('#mugrSearchModal'));
  }

  function esc(text){
    return String(text == null ? '' : text).replace(/[&<>'"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;',"'":'&#39;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});
  }
  function ready(fn){document.readyState==='loading'?document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',fn):fn();}
  function initRefs(){
    modal = document.getElementById('mugrSearchModal');
    if(!modal) return false;

    /* V3 Final: keep exactly one close button even if the modal markup
       is accidentally included twice by a legacy page template. */
    var closeButtons = modal.querySelectorAll('.mugr-search-dialog > .mugr-search-close');
    if(closeButtons.length > 1){
      Array.prototype.slice.call(closeButtons, 1).forEach(function(btn){
        if(btn && btn.parentNode){ btn.parentNode.removeChild(btn); }
      });
    }

    input = modal.querySelector('[data-mugr-modal-search-input]') || document.getElementById('mugrSearchInput');
    resultsBox = modal.querySelector('[data-mugr-search-results]') || document.getElementById('mugrSearchSuggest');
    statusBox = modal.querySelector('[data-mugr-search-status]');
    form = modal.querySelector('[data-mugr-modal-search-form]');
    lang = modal.getAttribute('data-lang') || 'thai';
    return true;
  }
  function mugrFullPageSearch(q){
    q = String(q || '').trim();
    var root = (modal && modal.getAttribute('data-lang') === 'inter') ? '/inter/' : '/thai/';
    var url = root + '?p=search';
    if(q) url += '&q=' + encodeURIComponent(q);
    window.location.assign(url);
  }

  function openModal(q, shouldSearch){
    /* Full-page Search 5.0: modal is retired on desktop and mobile. */
    mugrFullPageSearch(q);
    return;
    if(!initRefs()) return;
    closeLegacySuggestions();
    q = String(q || '').trim();
    modal.classList.add('is-open');
    modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
    document.body.classList.add('mugr-search-open');
    setTimeout(function(){
      if(input){
        input.focus();
        if(q){ input.value=q; input.select(); }
      }
    },60);
    if(q && shouldSearch === true){ runSearch(q); }
    else { renderTypingState(q); }
  }
  function closeModal(){
    activeSearchId++;
    stopLoadingExperience();
    closeLegacySuggestions();
    if(!modal) return;
    modal.classList.remove('is-open');
    modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    document.body.classList.remove('mugr-search-open');
  }
  function stopLoadingExperience(){
    if(loadingTimer){ clearInterval(loadingTimer); loadingTimer = null; }
  }
  function loadingTips(){
    return lang==='inter' ? [
      'Tip: You can search in Thai or English.',
      'Tip: Try abbreviations such as MUCE, IELTS or TOEFL.',
      'Tip: Search by announcement title, menu name, or document keyword.',
      'Tip: Results may include menus, pages and documents.',
      'Tip: Short keywords often find more results.'
    ] : [
      'เคล็ดลับ: ค้นหาได้ทั้งภาษาไทยและภาษาอังกฤษ',
      'เคล็ดลับ: ลองค้นด้วยคำย่อ เช่น MUCE, IELTS หรือ TOEFL',
      'เคล็ดลับ: ค้นจากชื่อประกาศจะเจอแม่นขึ้น',
      'เคล็ดลับ: ระบบค้นได้ทั้งเมนู หน้าเว็บ และเอกสาร',
      'เคล็ดลับ: ใช้คำค้นสั้น ๆ มักเจอผลลัพธ์กว้างกว่า'
    ];
  }
  function pickLoadingTip(){
    var tips = loadingTips();
    return tips[Math.floor(Math.random() * tips.length)] || tips[0] || '';
  }
  function loadingPipeline(){
    return lang==='inter' ? [
      {icon:'home', label:'Hero Banner', text:'Checking Hero Banner', count:'1'},
      {icon:'list', label:'Feature Menu', text:'Checking Feature Menu', count:'25'},
      {icon:'globe', label:'HTML / PHP Pages', text:'Searching website pages', count:'1,314'},
      {icon:'file-pdf-o', label:'PDF Documents', text:'Preparing document search', count:'soon'},
      {icon:'sort-amount-desc', label:'Ranking', text:'Ranking relevant results', count:'active'},
      {icon:'magic', label:'Finish', text:'Preparing results', count:'done'}
    ] : [
      {icon:'home', label:'Hero Banner', text:'ตรวจสอบ Hero Banner', count:'1'},
      {icon:'list', label:'Feature Menu', text:'ตรวจสอบ Feature Menu', count:'25'},
      {icon:'globe', label:'HTML / PHP Pages', text:'ค้นหาหน้าเว็บไซต์', count:'1,314'},
      {icon:'file-pdf-o', label:'PDF Documents', text:'เตรียมค้นหาเอกสาร PDF', count:'เร็ว ๆ นี้'},
      {icon:'sort-amount-desc', label:'Ranking', text:'จัดอันดับผลลัพธ์', count:'กำลังทำงาน'},
      {icon:'magic', label:'Finish', text:'กำลังเตรียมผลลัพธ์', count:'เสร็จแล้ว'}
    ];
  }
  function loadingTrafficClass(step){
    if(step <= 1) return ['is-red','',''];
    if(step <= 3) return ['','is-yellow',''];
    return ['','','is-green'];
  }
  function loadingEta(){
    var remain = Math.max(0.2, (100 - Math.min(loadingPercent, 99)) / 100).toFixed(1);
    if(loadingPercent >= 92){ return lang==='inter' ? 'Showing results...' : 'กำลังแสดงผล...'; }
    return lang==='inter' ? ('About ' + remain + ' sec left') : ('เหลือประมาณ ' + remain + ' วินาที');
  }
  function updateLoadingExperience(q){
    if(!resultsBox) return;
    var step = Math.min(loadingStep, 5);
    var lights = loadingTrafficClass(step);
    var title = lang==='inter' ? 'Searching' : 'กำลังค้นหา';
    var animatedDots = new Array(Math.max(1, Math.min(3, loadingDotCount)) + 1).join('.');
    var subtitle = (lang==='inter'
      ? 'Searching websites, documents and databases'
      : 'กำลังค้นหาจากเว็บไซต์ เอกสาร และฐานข้อมูล') + animatedDots;
    var percentText = Math.min(loadingPercent, 96) + '%';
    resultsBox.innerHTML =
      '<div class="mugr-search-loading-v40 mugr-search-loading-premium-v80" role="status" aria-live="polite">' +
        '<div class="mugr-search-loading-top-v70">' +
          '<div class="mugr-search-loading-icon-v70"><i class="fa fa-search" aria-hidden="true"></i></div>' +
          '<div><div class="mugr-search-loading-title-v70">' + esc(title) + ' “' + esc(q) + '”</div>' +
          '<div class="mugr-search-loading-sub-v70">' + esc(subtitle) + '</div></div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="mugr-search-traffic-v70" aria-hidden="true">' +
          '<span class="mugr-search-light-v70 '+lights[0]+'"></span>' +
          '<span class="mugr-search-light-v70 '+lights[1]+'"></span>' +
          '<span class="mugr-search-light-v70 '+lights[2]+'"></span>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="mugr-search-progress-v70"><div class="mugr-search-progress-bar-v70" style="width:'+percentText+'"></div></div>' +
        '<div class="mugr-search-loading-note-v70"><span>' + esc(loadingEta()) + '</span><span class="mugr-search-loading-percent-v70">' + percentText + '</span></div>' +
        '<div class="mugr-search-tip-v80"><i class="fa fa-lightbulb-o" aria-hidden="true"></i> '+esc(loadingTip)+'</div>' +
      '</div>';
  }
  function startLoadingExperience(q){
    stopLoadingExperience();
    loadingStartedAt = Date.now();
    loadingStep = 0;
    loadingPercent = 14;
    loadingDotCount = 1;
    loadingTip = pickLoadingTip();
    updateLoadingExperience(q);
    loadingTimer = setInterval(function(){
      loadingStep = Math.min(loadingStep + 1, loadingPipeline().length - 1);
      loadingDotCount = loadingDotCount >= 3 ? 1 : loadingDotCount + 1;
      var increments = [17, 18, 16, 13, 10, 5];
      loadingPercent = Math.min(96, loadingPercent + (increments[loadingStep] || 8));
      updateLoadingExperience(q);
    }, 420);
  }
  function afterMinimumLoading(callback){
    var elapsed = Date.now() - loadingStartedAt;
    var wait = Math.max(0, MIN_LOADING_VISIBLE_MS - elapsed);
    window.setTimeout(function(){
      if(callback) callback();
    }, wait);
  }
  function renderFinish(q, count, callback){
    stopLoadingExperience();
    if(!resultsBox){ if(callback) callback(); return; }
    var foundText = lang==='inter' ? ('Found ' + count + ' results') : ('พบ ' + count + ' รายการ');
    resultsBox.innerHTML =
      '<div class="mugr-search-loading-v40 mugr-search-finish-v80" role="status" aria-live="polite">' +
        '<div class="mugr-search-loading-top-v70">' +
          '<div class="mugr-search-loading-icon-v70 is-finish"><i class="fa fa-check" aria-hidden="true"></i></div>' +
          '<div><div class="mugr-search-loading-title-v70">' + esc(foundText) + '</div>' +
          '<div class="mugr-search-loading-sub-v70">' + esc(lang==='inter'?'Preparing the best matches for you.':'กำลังจัดเตรียมผลลัพธ์ที่เหมาะสมที่สุด') + '</div></div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="mugr-search-traffic-v70" aria-hidden="true"><span></span><span></span><span class="is-green"></span></div>' +
        '<div class="mugr-search-progress-v70"><div class="mugr-search-progress-bar-v70" style="width:100%"></div></div>' +
        '<div class="mugr-search-loading-note-v70"><span>✨ '+esc(foundText)+'</span><span class="mugr-search-loading-percent-v70">100%</span></div>' +
      '</div>';
    setTimeout(function(){ if(callback) callback(); }, 280);
  }
  function renderTypingState(q){
    stopLoadingExperience();
    q = String(q || '').trim();
    if(statusBox){
      statusBox.textContent = q
        ? (lang==='inter' ? 'Ready to search. Press Enter or click Search.' : 'พร้อมค้นหา กด Enter หรือปุ่ม Search เพื่อเริ่มค้นหา')
        : (lang==='inter' ? 'Type a keyword, then press Enter or click Search.' : 'พิมพ์คำค้น แล้วกด Enter หรือปุ่ม Search เพื่อเริ่มค้นหา');
    }
    if(resultsBox){ resultsBox.innerHTML = ''; }
  }
  function renderLoading(q){
    if(statusBox) statusBox.textContent = (lang==='inter'?'Searching for ':'กำลังค้นหา ') + '“' + q + '”';
    startLoadingExperience(q);
  }
  function renderEmpty(q){
    stopLoadingExperience();
    if(statusBox) statusBox.textContent = lang==='inter' ? 'No results found.' : 'ไม่พบข้อมูลที่ตรงกับคำค้นหา';
    if(resultsBox) resultsBox.innerHTML = '<div class="mugr-search-empty-v40"><strong>'+(lang==='inter'?'No results found':'ไม่พบข้อมูล')+'</strong><br><span>'+(lang==='inter'?'Try another keyword or a shorter word.':'ลองเปลี่ยนคำค้น หรือใช้คำที่สั้นลง')+'</span></div>';
  }
  function highlightText(text,q){
    var safe = esc(text || '');
    q = String(q || '').trim();
    if(!q) return safe;
    try{
      var words = q.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).map(function(w){return w.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&');});
      if(!words.length) return safe;
      return safe.replace(new RegExp('(' + words.join('|') + ')','ig'), '<span class="mugr-search-mark">$1</span>');
    }catch(e){ return safe; }
  }
  function normalizePersonKeyV61(text){
    return String(text || '').toLowerCase().replace(/<[^>]*>/g,' ').replace(/[^a-z0-9ก-๙@._+-]+/gu,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
  }
  function extractEmailV61(text){
    var m=String(text||'').match(/[A-Z0-9._%+\-]+@[A-Z0-9.\-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/i);
    return m ? m[0].toLowerCase() : '';
  }
  function personIdentityV61(item){
    item=item||{};
    var blob=[item.title,item.description,item.email,item.url].join(' ');
    var email=extractEmailV61(blob);
    if(email) return 'email:'+email;
    return 'name:'+normalizePersonKeyV61(item.title||'');
  }
  function sameAsAnswerV61(item,answer){
    if(!item||!answer) return false;
    var answerType=String(answer.answer_type||'').toLowerCase();
    var itemType=String(item.entity_type||'').toLowerCase();
    if(answerType!=='person' && answerType!=='programme_director') return false;
    if(itemType && itemType!=='person' && itemType!=='programme_director') return false;
    var aKey=personIdentityV61(answer), iKey=personIdentityV61(item);
    if(aKey && iKey && aKey===iKey) return true;
    var aTitle=normalizePersonKeyV61(answer.title||''), iTitle=normalizePersonKeyV61(item.title||'');
    return !!(aTitle && iTitle && aTitle===iTitle);
  }
  function answerAvatarHtmlV61(answer){
    var type=String(answer&&answer.answer_type||'').toLowerCase();
    var icon=esc((answer&&answer.icon)||'user');
    var fallback='<span class="mugr-answer-avatar-fallback-v61"><i class="fa fa-'+icon+'" aria-hidden="true"></i></span>';
    if(type!=='person' && type!=='programme_director') return '<div class="mugr-answer-icon-v60">'+fallback+'</div>';
    var url=String((answer&&answer.url)||'');
    var m=url.match(/#staff-([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/i);
    if(!m) return '<div class="mugr-answer-icon-v60">'+fallback+'</div>';
    var photo='/assets61/img/pic_staff/'+encodeURIComponent(m[1])+'.jpg';
    return '<div class="mugr-answer-icon-v60"><img src="'+esc(photo)+'" alt="'+esc((answer&&answer.title)||'')+'" loading="lazy" onload="this.parentNode.classList.add(\'has-photo\')" onerror="this.remove()">'+fallback+'</div>';
  }
  function answerHtml(answer,q){
    if(!answer || !answer.title) return '';
    var actions=(answer.actions||[]).map(function(a){
      var target=/^https?:\/\//i.test(a.url||'')?' target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"':'';
      return '<a class="mugr-answer-action-v60'+(a.primary?' is-primary':'')+'" href="'+esc(a.url||'#')+'"'+target+'><i class="fa fa-'+(a.primary?'arrow-right':'envelope-o')+'" aria-hidden="true"></i>'+esc(a.label||'')+'</a>';
    }).join('');
    var facts='';
    if(answer.email) facts+='<span class="mugr-answer-fact-v60"><i class="fa fa-envelope-o"></i>'+esc(answer.email)+'</span>';
    if(Number(answer.related_programme_count||0)>0) facts+='<span class="mugr-answer-fact-v60"><i class="fa fa-graduation-cap"></i>'+esc(lang==='inter'?(answer.related_programme_count+' related programmes'):('เกี่ยวข้อง '+answer.related_programme_count+' หลักสูตร'))+'</span>';
    var related=(answer.related||[]).map(function(r){return '<a class="mugr-answer-related-item-v60" href="'+esc(r.url||'#')+'">'+esc(r.title||'')+'</a>';}).join('');
    return '<section class="mugr-answer-card-v60" data-answer-type="'+esc(answer.answer_type||'')+'">'+
      '<div class="mugr-answer-top-v60">'+answerAvatarHtmlV61(answer)+'<div>'+
      '<span class="mugr-answer-kicker-v60">'+esc(answer.badge||(lang==='inter'?'Best answer':'คำตอบที่แนะนำ'))+'</span>'+
      '<div class="mugr-answer-title-v60">'+highlightText(answer.title||'',q)+'</div>'+
      (answer.subtitle?'<div class="mugr-answer-subtitle-v60">'+esc(answer.subtitle)+'</div>':'')+
      (answer.description?'<div class="mugr-answer-desc-v60">'+highlightText(answer.description,q)+'</div>':'')+
      (facts?'<div class="mugr-answer-facts-v60">'+facts+'</div>':'')+
      (actions?'<div class="mugr-answer-actions-v60">'+actions+'</div>':'')+
      (related?'<div class="mugr-answer-related-v60"><strong>'+(lang==='inter'?'Related information':'ข้อมูลที่เกี่ยวข้อง')+'</strong><div class="mugr-answer-related-list-v60">'+related+'</div></div>':'')+
      '</div></div></section>';
  }
  function modernAbstractUrl(url){
    url=String(url||'');
    if(!/abstract_view\.php/i.test(url)) return url;
    try{
      var parsed=new URL(url, window.location.origin);
      var id=(parsed.searchParams.get('id')||'').trim();
      if(!id) return url;
      var fac=(parsed.searchParams.get('fac')||'').trim();
      var prg=(parsed.searchParams.get('prg')||'').trim();
      var gp=(parsed.searchParams.get('gp')||'').trim();
      var root=(lang==='inter')?'/inter':'/thai';
      var q=new URLSearchParams();
      q.set('g','3');
      if(gp) q.set('t', String(parseInt(gp,10)||gp));
      if(fac) q.set('fid',fac);
      if(prg) q.set('cid',prg);
      q.set('abstract',id);
      return root+'/current-students/?'+q.toString();
    }catch(e){ return url; }
  }
  function cardHtml(item,q){
    var url = modernAbstractUrl(item.url || '#');
    var target = /^https?:\/\//i.test(url) ? ' target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"' : '';
    var icon = item.icon || ((item.entity_type==='programme_director'||item.entity_type==='person')?'user':(item.entity_type==='scholarship'?'gift':'search'));
    var meta = '';
    if(item.display_badge||item.category||item.type) meta += '<span>'+esc(item.display_badge||item.category||item.type)+'</span>';
    if(item.scholarship_category) meta += '<span class="is-scholarship-category">'+esc(item.scholarship_category)+'</span>';
    if(item.year_label) meta += '<span class="is-scholarship-year">'+esc(item.year_label)+'</span>';
    if(item.status_text){
      var statusClass = String(item.status_key||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g,'');
      meta += '<span class="is-status-'+esc(statusClass||'closed')+'">'+esc(item.status_text)+'</span>';
    }
    if(item.score) meta += '<em>score '+esc(item.score)+'</em>';
    return '<a class="mugr-search-result-card-v40" href="'+esc(url)+'"'+target+' data-mugr-search-click data-query="'+esc(q)+'">'+
      '<div><div class="mugr-search-result-title-v40"><i class="fa fa-'+esc(icon)+'" aria-hidden="true"></i> '+highlightText(item.title||'',q)+'</div>'+
      (item.description?'<div class="mugr-search-result-desc-v40">'+highlightText(item.description,q)+'</div>':'')+
      '<div class="mugr-search-result-meta-v40">'+meta+'</div></div>'+
      '<span class="mugr-search-result-arrow-v40">›</span></a>';
  }
  function renderItems(data,q){
    stopLoadingExperience();
    var answer = (data&&data.answer)?data.answer:null;
    var items = (data && data.items) ? data.items.slice() : [];
    var groups = (data && data.groups) ? data.groups.map(function(group){
      var copy={}; Object.keys(group||{}).forEach(function(k){copy[k]=group[k];});
      copy.items=(group&&group.items?group.items:[]).slice();
      return copy;
    }) : [];

    // V61: an Answer Card is a promoted copy of the best result. Remove that
    // same person from the normal list so a staff member is shown only once.
    if(answer){
      items=items.filter(function(item){return !sameAsAnswerV61(item,answer);});
      groups=groups.map(function(group){
        group.items=(group.items||[]).filter(function(item){return !sameAsAnswerV61(item,answer);});
        group.count=group.items.length;
        return group;
      }).filter(function(group){return group.items.length>0;});
    }
    var visibleCount=items.length + (answer?1:0);
    if(!visibleCount){ renderEmpty(q); return; }
    if(statusBox){
      statusBox.textContent = (lang==='inter'?'Found ':'พบ ') + visibleCount + (lang==='inter'?' results for ':' รายการสำหรับ ') + '“' + q + '”';
    }
    var answerMarkup = answerHtml(answer,q);
    if(groups.length){
      resultsBox.innerHTML = answerMarkup + groups.map(function(group){
        var gItems = group.items || [];
        var icon = group.icon || 'search';
        return '<section class="mugr-search-group-v80">'+
          '<div class="mugr-search-group-head-v80"><span><i class="fa fa-'+esc(icon)+'" aria-hidden="true"></i>'+esc(group.display_badge||group.category||'Other')+'</span><span class="mugr-search-group-count-v80">'+esc(group.count||gItems.length)+'</span></div>'+
          gItems.map(function(item){ return cardHtml(item,q); }).join('')+
        '</section>';
      }).join('');
    }else{
      resultsBox.innerHTML = answerMarkup + items.map(function(item){ return cardHtml(item,q); }).join('');
    }
  }
  function runSearch(q){
    closeLegacySuggestions();
    q = String(q||'').trim();
    if(!q){ return; }

    /* Record an actual submitted search. Input typing is not recorded. */
    try{
      var searchFd = new FormData();
      searchFd.append('q', q);
      searchFd.append('type', 'search');
      searchFd.append('lang', lang);
      if(navigator.sendBeacon){
        navigator.sendBeacon('/engine/search/v5/api/track.php', searchFd);
      }else{
        fetch('/engine/search/v5/api/track.php',{
          method:'POST',
          body:searchFd,
          credentials:'same-origin',
          keepalive:true
        }).catch(function(){});
      }
    }catch(trackError){}

    var searchId = ++activeSearchId;
    renderLoading(q);
    if(lastController && lastController.abort) lastController.abort();
    lastController = window.AbortController ? new AbortController() : null;
    var url = endpoint + '?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q) + '&lang=' + encodeURIComponent(lang) + '&v=50';
    function fetchSearch(urlToFetch){
      return fetch(urlToFetch,{signal:lastController?lastController.signal:undefined, credentials:'same-origin'})
        .then(function(r){ if(!r.ok) throw new Error('search-http-'+r.status); return r.json(); })
        .then(function(data){ if(!data || !Array.isArray(data.items)) throw new Error('search-invalid-json'); return data; });
    }
    fetchSearch(url)
      .then(function(data){
        afterMinimumLoading(function(){
          if(searchId !== activeSearchId) return;
          var items=(data&&data.items)?data.items:[];
          if(items.length){
            renderFinish(q, items.length, function(){
              if(searchId === activeSearchId) renderItems(data,q);
            });
          }else{
            renderItems(data,q);
          }
        });
      })
      .catch(function(err){
        if(err && err.name==='AbortError') return;
        afterMinimumLoading(function(){
          if(searchId !== activeSearchId) return;
          stopLoadingExperience();
          renderEmpty(q);
        });
      });
  }
  /* Search Framework V3 public controller.
     All header/page search forms use this one modal entry point, including
     premium loading/countdown and Universal Search results. */
  window.MUGRSearchModalV3 = {
    open: function(q, shouldSearch){ mugrFullPageSearch(q); },
    search: function(q){ mugrFullPageSearch(q); },
    close: function(){ closeModal(); },
    version: '5.0-phase4-safe-integration'
  };

  var timer;
  ready(function(){
    if(!initRefs()) return;

    // Keep the browser's native Ctrl+F / Cmd+F page-find behavior.
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      if(e.key === 'Escape'){ closeModal(); return; }
      if(e.key !== 'Enter') return;
      var t = e.target;
      if(!isSearchInput(t)) return;
      if(isDirectSearch(t)) return;
      var q = String(t.value || '').trim();
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      if(e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      openModal(q, true);
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener('submit', function(e){
      var f = e.target;
      if(!f || !f.querySelector) return;
      var qInput = f.querySelector(SEARCH_INPUT_SELECTOR);
      if(!qInput) return;
      if(isInsideModal(f) || isInsideModal(qInput)) return;
      if(isDirectSearch(f) || isDirectSearch(qInput)) return;
      var q = String(qInput.value || '').trim();
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      if(e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      openModal(q, true);
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
      var openBtn = e.target.closest('[data-mugr-search-open], .mugr-header-search-trigger, .u-search-open, .search-open');
      if(openBtn){ e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); closeLegacySuggestions(); mugrFullPageSearch(''); return; }
      if(e.target.closest('[data-mugr-search-close]')){ e.preventDefault(); closeModal(); return; }
      var resultLink = e.target.closest('[data-mugr-search-click]');
      if(resultLink){
        try{
          var fd = new FormData();
          fd.append('q', resultLink.getAttribute('data-query') || (input ? input.value : ''));
          fd.append('type', 'click');
          fd.append('url', resultLink.getAttribute('href') || '');
          if(navigator.sendBeacon){ navigator.sendBeacon('/engine/search/v5/api/track.php', fd); }
        }catch(err){}
        return;
      }
      var chip = e.target.closest('[data-search-keyword]');
      if(chip){ e.preventDefault(); var q=chip.getAttribute('data-search-keyword')||''; mugrFullPageSearch(q); }
    }, true);

    if(form){
      form.addEventListener('submit',function(e){
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        if(e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();
        runSearch(input ? input.value : '');
      }, true);
    }
    if(input){
      input.addEventListener('input',function(){
        clearTimeout(timer);
        renderTypingState(input.value || '');
      });
    }

    closeLegacySuggestions();
  });
})();
</script>

<script id="mugr-search-fullpage-route-v1">
(function(){
  function langRoot(){var el=document.getElementById('mugrSearchModal');return el&&el.getAttribute('data-lang')==='inter'?'/inter/':'/thai/';}
  function go(q){q=(q||'').trim();var u=langRoot()+'?p=search';if(q)u+='&q='+encodeURIComponent(q);window.location.assign(u);}
  document.addEventListener('submit',function(e){
    var f=e.target;if(!f||!(f.matches&&f.matches('[data-mugr-modal-search-form],[data-mugr-search-inline-form],[data-mugr-global-search-form]')))return;
    e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();var inp=f.querySelector('input[name="q"]');go(inp?inp.value:'');
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var chip=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('.mugr-search-chip'):null;
    if(chip){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();go(chip.getAttribute('data-keyword')||chip.textContent||'');}
  },true);
  window.MUGRSearchModalV3={open:function(q){go(q||'');},close:function(){},search:function(q){go(q||'');}};
})();
</script>

</style><div id="mugrCookieBar" class="mugr-cookie-mu" hidden><div class="mugr-cookie-mu__inner"><div class="mugr-cookie-mu__text"><span>เว็บไซต์นี้ใช้คุกกี้เพื่อทำให้คุณมีประสบการณ์ที่ดีในการใช้งานเว็บไซต์ โปรดคลิก “ยอมรับ” หรือคลิกการตั้งค่าคุกกี้ <a data-mugr-cookie="necessary" aria-label="ปิดโดยใช้เฉพาะคุกกี้ที่จำเป็น">×</button></div></div></div><div id="mugrCookieBackdrop" class="mugr-cookie-backdrop" hidden></div><section id="mugrCookieModal" data-mugr-cookie="modal-close" aria-label="Close">×</button><h3 id="mugrCookieTitle">เว็บไซต์นี้ใช้คุกกี้</h3><p class="mugr-cookie-modal__intro">เราใช้คุกกี้เพื่อปรับปรุงประสบการณ์การใช้งานเว็บไซต์</p><a class="mugr-cookie-modal__more" href="/thai/?p=termAndPrivacy&amp;g=1">Show more</a><details class="mugr-cookie-detail"><summary><span class="mugr-cookie-detail__title">คุกกี้ที่จำเป็น</span><span class="mugr-cookie-detail__state">Always Enabled</span></summary><p class="mugr-cookie-detail__desc">คุกกี้ที่จำเป็น คือคุกกี้ที่จำเป็นต้องมีเพื่อให้เว็บไซต์ทำงานได้อย่างถูกต้อง คุกกี้เหล่านี้ช่วยให้มั่นใจถึงฟังก์ชันพื้นฐานและคุณลักษณะด้านความปลอดภัยของเว็บไซต์</p></details><details class="mugr-cookie-detail"><summary><span class="mugr-cookie-detail__title">คุกกี้เพื่อการวิเคราะห์</span><span id="mugrCookieAnalyticsState" class="mugr-cookie-detail__state">Disabled</span><label class="mugr-cookie-switch" onclick="event.stopPropagation()"><input id="mugrCookieAnalytics" type="checkbox"><span></span></label></summary><p class="mugr-cookie-detail__desc">คุกกี้เพื่อการวิเคราะห์/เพื่อประสิทธิภาพ ช่วยให้เราทราบจำนวนผู้เข้าชมเว็บไซต์และรูปแบบการใช้งาน เพื่อนำไปปรับปรุงการทำงานของเว็บไซต์ให้ดียิ่งขึ้นในอนาคต</p></details><div class="mugr-cookie-modal__footer"><button type="button" class="mugr-cookie-save" data-mugr-cookie="save">Save & Accept</button></div></div></section><script>(function(){var VERSION="2.3",MODE="consent",NAME="MUGR_COOKIE_CONSENT",bar=document.getElementById("mugrCookieBar"),modal=document.getElementById("mugrCookieModal"),back=document.getElementById("mugrCookieBackdrop"),analytics=document.getElementById("mugrCookieAnalytics"),state=document.getElementById("mugrCookieAnalyticsState");function read(n){var m=document.cookie.match(new RegExp("(?:^|; )"+n.replace(/([.$?*|{}()\[\]\\\/+^])/g,"\$1")+"=([^;]*)"));return m?decodeURIComponent(m[1]):""}function write(n,v,days){var max=days*86400;document.cookie=n+"="+encodeURIComponent(v)+"; path=/; max-age="+max+"; SameSite=Lax"}function parse(){var raw=read(NAME);if(!raw)return null;try{return JSON.parse(raw)}catch(e){return null}}function valid(c){return c&&String(c.version)===String(VERSION)}function updateState(){if(state)state.textContent=analytics&&analytics.checked?"Enabled":"Disabled"}function cid(){var k="mugr-consent-id",v="";try{v=localStorage.getItem(k)||"";if(!v){v=(crypto&&crypto.randomUUID)?crypto.randomUUID():Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);localStorage.setItem(k,v)}}catch(e){v="anon"}return v}function track(action,a){try{var body=JSON.stringify({action:action,analytics:a?1:0,version:VERSION,lang:(document.documentElement.lang||"th").slice(0,2),device:innerWidth<768?"mobile":(innerWidth<1200?"tablet":"desktop"),cid:cid()});fetch("/engine/structure/cookie-consent-event.php",{method:"POST",headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json"},credentials:"same-origin",keepalive:true,body:body})}catch(e){}}function activateAnalytics(){document.querySelectorAll("script[type=\"text/plain\"][data-mugr-consent=\"analytics\"]").forEach(function(old){if(old.dataset.activated)return;var n=document.createElement("script");Array.from(old.attributes).forEach(function(a){if(a.name!=="type"&&a.name!=="data-mugr-consent")n.setAttribute(a.name,a.value)});n.text=old.text||old.textContent;old.dataset.activated="1";old.parentNode.insertBefore(n,old.nextSibling)})}function save(a,action){var c={version:VERSION,necessary:1,analytics:a?1:0,updated:(new Date()).toISOString()};write(NAME,JSON.stringify(c),365);write("MUGRAD_PDPA","1",365);try{localStorage.setItem("mugr-cookie-consent",JSON.stringify(c));localStorage.setItem("mugr-cookie-ok","1")}catch(e){}bar.hidden=true;closeModal();track(action||"save",a);if(a)activateAnalytics();document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("mugr:cookie-consent",{detail:c}));return c}function openModal(){var c=parse();analytics.checked=!!(c&&c.analytics);updateState();modal.hidden=false;back.hidden=false;document.documentElement.style.overflow="hidden"}function closeModal(){modal.hidden=true;back.hidden=true;document.documentElement.style.overflow=""}var c=parse();if(!valid(c)){var legacy=read("MUGRAD_PDPA");if(legacy){c=save(true,"legacy")}else{bar.hidden=false;track("shown",false)}}else{bar.hidden=true;if(c.analytics)activateAnalytics()}if(analytics)analytics.addEventListener("change",updateState);var cookieDetails=modal?modal.querySelectorAll(".mugr-cookie-detail"):[];cookieDetails.forEach(function(d){d.addEventListener("toggle",function(){if(!d.open)return;cookieDetails.forEach(function(other){if(other!==d&&other.open)other.open=false})})});document.addEventListener("click",function(e){var t=e.target.closest("[data-mugr-cookie]");if(!t)return;var a=t.getAttribute("data-mugr-cookie");if(a==="settings")openModal();else if(a==="accept")save(true,"accept_all");else if(a==="necessary")save(false,"necessary_only");else if(a==="save")save(MODE==="notice"?true:analytics.checked,"save_settings");else if(a==="modal-close")closeModal()});back.addEventListener("click",closeModal);document.addEventListener("keydown",function(e){if(e.key==="Escape"&&!modal.hidden)closeModal()});window.MUGRCookie={open:openModal,get:parse,accept:function(){save(true,"api_accept")},necessary:function(){save(false,"api_necessary")},analyticsAllowed:function(){var c=parse();return !!(valid(c)&&c.analytics)}}})();</script>
